- **seiche-regime-watch**: Alerting policy for the Seiche funding-stress board. Encodes when to speak and, just as important, when to stay silent. ([beepboop2025/seiche](https://github.com/beepboop2025/seiche/tree/HEAD/integrations/hermes/skills/seiche-regime-watch) · ★1)
- **seiche-time-machine**: Point-in-time replay of the Seiche board for historical study. Enforces no-lookahead discipline. ([beepboop2025/seiche](https://github.com/beepboop2025/seiche/tree/HEAD/integrations/hermes/skills/seiche-time-machine) · ★1)
- **seija-assumption-inversion**: Expose hidden assumptions in a consensus, strategy, requirement, or design by stating the default precisely, inverting one assumption at a time, constructing the strongest falsifiable counterexample, and reconciling the result with evidence. Do not use to deny established facts, manufacture controversy, or replace rule-based review.
